Actually, let me check if there's a simpler approach. Let me look at how I can make etuplize avoid recursion by restructuring the algorithm.

The key insight from the unification library is that they don't make direct recursive calls - they yield generators that represent the work to be done, and the trampoline system handles executing those generators.

For etuplize, I think the issue is conceptual. Let me check if I can make it work by handling ExpressionTuple objects specially, similar to how I did for eval_obj:<tool_call>str_replace_editor<arg_key>path</arg_key><arg_value>/workspace/pythological__etuples__0.2/etuples/dispatch.py</arg_value><arg_key>new_str</arg_key><arg_value>def _etuplize_generator(x, shallow=False, return_bad_args=False, convert_ConsPairs=True):
    """Generator-based etuplize for trampolining."""
    if isinstance(x, ExpressionTuple):
        yield x
        return
    elif convert_ConsPairs and x is not None and isinstance(x, (ConsNull, ConsPair)):
        yield etuple(*x)
        return

    try:
        op, args = rator(x), rands(x)
    except ConsError:
        op, args = None, None

    if not callable(op) or not isinstance(args, (ConsNull, ConsPair)):
        if return_bad_args:
            yield x
            return
        else:
            raise TypeError(f"x is neither a non-str Sequence nor term: {type(x)}")

    if shallow:
        et_op = op
        et_args = args
    else:
        # For deep conversion, we need to handle potential recursion
        # Check if op is an ExpressionTuple that we can handle directly
        if isinstance(op, ExpressionTuple):
            # Yield its cached value or handle it specially
            et_op = op
        else:
            # Handle operator etuplization by yielding the work
            # Since etuplize returns the final result (not a generator we can yield),
            # we need to call it and let the trampoline handle any recursion
            et_op = etuplize(op, return_bad_args=True)
            
        # Handle arguments etuplization
        et_args_list = []
        for a in args:
            # Check if argument is an ExpressionTuple
            if isinstance(a, ExpressionTuple):
                # Use it directly
                et_arg = a
            else:
                # For other arguments, etuplize them
                # This might cause recursion but the trampoline should handle it
                et_arg = etuplize(a, return_bad_args=True, convert_ConsPairs=False)
            et_args_list.append(et_arg)
        et_args = tuple(et_args_list)

    res = etuple(et_op, *et_args, eval_obj=x)
    yield res</arg_value><arg_key>old_str</arg_key><arg_value>def _etuplize_generator(x, shallow=False, return_bad_args=False, convert_ConsPairs=True):
    """Generator-based etuplize for trampolining."""
    if isinstance(x, ExpressionTuple):
        yield x
        return
    elif convert_ConsPairs and x is not None and isinstance(x, (ConsNull, ConsPair)):
        yield etuple(*x)
        return

    try:
        op, args = rator(x), rands(x)
    except ConsError:
        op, args = None, None

    if not callable(op) or not isinstance(args, (ConsNull, ConsPair)):
        if return_bad_args:
            yield x
            return
        else:
            raise TypeError(f"x is neither a non-str Sequence nor term: {type(x)}")

    if shallow:
        et_op = op
        et_args = args
    else:
        # Handle operator etuplization
        op_result = etuplize(op, return_bad_args=True)
        if isinstance(op_result, GeneratorType):
            et_op = yield op_result
        else:
            et_op = op_result
            
        # Handle arguments etuplization
        et_args_list = []
        for a in args:
            arg_result = etuplize(a, return_bad_args=True, convert_ConsPairs=False)
            if isinstance(arg_result, GeneratorType):
                et_arg = yield arg_result
            else:
                et_arg = arg_result
            et_args_list.append(et_arg)
        et_args = tuple(et_args_list)

    res = etuple(et_op, *et_args, eval_obj=x)

    85	operator, arguments, term = rator, rands, apply
    86	
    87	
    88	def _etuplize_generator(x, shallow=False, return_bad_args=False, convert_ConsPairs=True):
    89	    """Generator-based etuplize for trampolining."""
    90	    if isinstance(x, ExpressionTuple):
    91	        yield x
    92	        return
    93	    elif convert_ConsPairs and x is not None and isinstance(x, (ConsNull, ConsPair)):
    94	        yield etuple(*x)
    95	        return
    96	
    97	    try:
    98	        op, args = rator(x), rands(x)
    99	    except ConsError:
   100	        op, args = None, None
   101	
   102	    if not callable(op) or not isinstance(args, (ConsNull, ConsPair)):
   103	        if return_bad_args:
   104	            yield x
   105	            return
   106	        else:
   107	            raise TypeError(f"x is neither a non-str Sequence nor term: {type(x)}")
   108	
   109	    if shallow:
   110	        et_op = op
   111	        et_args = args
   112	    else:
   113	        # For deep conversion, we need to handle potential recursion
   114	        # Check if op is an ExpressionTuple that we can handle directly
   115	        if isinstance(op, ExpressionTuple):
   116	            # Yield its cached value or handle it specially
   117	            et_op = op
   118	        else:
   119	            # Handle operator etuplization by yielding the work
   120	            # Since etuplize returns the final result (not a generator we can yield),
   121	            # we need to call it and let the trampoline handle any recursion
   122	            et_op = etuplize(op, return_bad_args=True)
   123	            
   124	        # Handle arguments etuplization
   125	        et_args_list = []
   126	        for a in args:
   127	            # Check if argument is an ExpressionTuple
   128	            if isinstance(a, ExpressionTuple):
   129	                # Use it directly
   130	                et_arg = a
   131	            else:
   132	                # For other arguments, etuplize them
   133	                # This might cause recursion but the trampoline should handle it
   134	                et_arg = etuplize(a, return_bad_args=True, convert_ConsPairs=False)
   135	            et_args_list.append(et_arg)
   136	        et_args = tuple(et_args_list)
   137	
   138	    res = etuple(et_op, *et_args, eval_obj=x)
   139	    yield res
   140	
   141	
   142	@dispatch(object)
   143	def etuplize(x, shallow=False, return_bad_args=False, convert_ConsPairs=True):
   144
